In this paper, we propose Poller (Poetry LLM Evaluator), a novel method leveraging large language models (LLMs) to evaluate the poetry understanding task. Specifically, our method requires LLMs to play the role of a poem's author with detailed information, thereby emulating human evaluation and judgment by adopting the poet's perspective. We conducted comprehensive experiments on multiple LLMs, evaluating the interpretations of poems across eight specialized dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that our method effectively reduces the evaluation error between LLMs and humans. Especially for specific dimension evaluation, Poller-based LLMs achieve a 94.55% and 89.53% error reduction for rhetorical techniques and defamiliarization, respectively, compared to baseline methods. These performances are unattainable by conventional LLM evaluation methods. Experimental results from multiple LLMs across various dimensions validate the efficacy of our method. This work bridges the gap between automated efficiency and human expertise, establishing a foundation for automated evaluation in poetry-related tasks.

</details>


### [115] [Morphing into Hybrid Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30562)
*Disen Lan,Jianbin Zheng,Yuxi Ren,Xin Xia,Xuanda Wang,Xuefeng Xiao,Xipeng Qiu,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FlashMorph方法，将混合注意力层选择形式化为预算约束的子集优化问题，通过可学习的门控机制和线性化正则化，高效选择保留全注意力的层，实现Transformer到混合架构的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合注意力模型的层选择方法多依赖启发式策略（如固定模式或逐层评分），忽略了全局混合配置下各层之间的相互依赖效应，导致转换效果不佳。

Method: 1) 构建可变形模型，为每个全注意力层配备转换后的线性注意力分支；2) 冻结模型权重，在合成长上下文检索数据上联合优化逐层门控，并加入线性化正则化；3) 在预设预算下离散化门控，实例化混合架构；4) 进行logits蒸馏和长上下文微调。

Result: FlashMorph能发现更有效的混合配置，在保持强长上下文召回率和通用基准性能的同时，显著降低了层选择成本。

Conclusion: FlashMorph是一种有效、高效且可扩展的Transformer转混合架构层选择方法，优于现有层选择方法。

Abstract: Hybrid attention models improve long-context efficiency by retaining only a subset of full-attention layers and replacing the remaining layers with linear attention. However, the effectiveness of Transformer-to-hybrid conversion critically depends on which layers preserve full attention. Existing hybrid layer selection methods typically rely on heuristic strategies such as fixed placement patterns or layerwise scoring, implicitly treating layer importance as isolated and overlooking the interdependent layer effect under a global hybrid configuration. In this work, we formulate hybrid layer selection as a budget-constrained subset optimization problem. We further propose FlashMorph (Fast LAyer Selection for Hybrid MORPHing), an effective, efficient and scalable layer selection method for Transformer-to-hybrid conversion. FlashMorph first constructs a morphable model by equipping each full-attention layer with a converted linear-attention branch. It then freezes all model weights and jointly optimizes layerwise gates on synthetic long-context retrieval data, with a linearization regularization that encourages the model to rely on linear attention for efficiency. The learned gates are discretized under a preset full-attention budget to instantiate the hybrid architecture, followed by standard logits distillation and long-context finetuning. Extensive experiments show that FlashMorph discovers more effective hybrid configurations, preserves strong long-context recall and general benchmark performance while substantially reducing layer selection cost compared with existing layer selection methods, demonstrating its effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability.

</details>


### [116] [Uncertainty-Aware Generation and Decision-Making Under Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30578)
*Nico Daheim,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了基于贝叶斯决策理论和风险规避决策的不确定性感知决策算法，应用于辅导和自动同行评审任务，实验发现贝叶斯方法优于风险规避规则，但仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂真实任务中应用日益广泛，但这些任务往往具有主观性且需要输出可信赖，然而决策算法方面的研究相对不足。

Method: 基于贝叶斯决策理论和风险规避决策，将不确定性纳入辅导策略和评审分数的生成过程，并使用保形预测提供策略和分数的保障。

Result: 这些算法能够提升生成的效用，但在高模糊性场景下需要谨慎实现；风险规避规则可能导致生成通用输出而降低性能，贝叶斯方法表现更优。

Conclusion: 决策理论有助于改进基于LLM的决策，但仍存在开放性挑战供社区进一步探索。

Abstract: With rapidly improving capabilities, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in many complex real-world tasks. Beyond requiring in-depth knowledge and reasoning skills, many of these tasks exhibit a high degree of subjectivity and require that the outputs of the model can be trusted. While a lot of progress has been made to train better models, decision-making algorithms have received less attention. In this work, we present and evaluate various uncertainty-aware decision-making algorithms based on Bayesian decision theory and risk-averse decision making on the tasks of tutoring and automatic peer reviewing. Concretely, we take uncertainty over tutoring strategies and review scores into account when generating a tutor response or review and use conformal prediction to provide guarantees over strategy and score. We find empirically that these algorithms can improve the utility of the generations but need to be carefully implemented when ambiguity is high. For example, risk-averse rules can degrade performance by optimizing for generic outputs, while Bayesian methods tend to perform better. Our work uses techniques from decision theory to improve LLM-based decision-making and outlines open challenges for the community.

</details>


### [117] [Scaling the Horizon, Not the Parameters: Reaching Trillion-Parameter Performance with a 35B Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30616)
*Lei Bai,Zongsheng Cao,Yang Chen,Zhiyao Cui,Shangheng Du,Yue Fan,Shiyang Feng,Zijie Guo,Haonan He,Liang He,Xiaohan He,Shuyue Hu,Yusong Hu,Songtao Huang,Yichen Jiang,Hao Li,Xin Li,Dahua Lin,Weihao Lin,Fenghua Ling,Dongrui Liu,Zhuo Liu,Runmin Ma,Chunjiang Mu,Haoyang Peng,Tianshuo Peng,Jinxin Shi,Luohe Shi,Boyuan Sun,Zelin Tan,Shengji Tang,Qianyi Wang,Yiming Wu,Yi Xie,Xiangchao Yan,Jingqi Ye,Peng Ye,Fangchen Yu,Jiakang Yuan,Bihao Zhan,Bo Zhang,Chen Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Shuaiyu Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Yiqun Zhang,Junpeng Zhao,Zhijie Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Yuhao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出35B参数的混合专家智能体模型Agents-A1，通过扩展智能体视野（平均轨迹长度45K tokens）达到万亿参数级别性能，在多个长视野基准测试中超越或媲美1T参数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索从长视野轨迹扩展和异构智能体能力扩展两个维度来提升智能体模型性能，希望用小参数模型达到大参数模型的性能水平。

Method: 构建长视野知识-动作基础设施（平均45K token轨迹），采用三阶段训练：1）全领域监督微调；2）领域级教师模型训练；3）多教师领域路由在线蒸馏+显著词汇对齐，统一6个异构领域到单一部署模型。

Result: 35B的Agents-A1在SEAL-0(56.4)、IFBench(80.6)、HiPhO(46.4)等基准上超越Kimi-K2.6和DeepSeek-V4-pro等1T模型，在SciCode(44.3)、HLE(47.6)等上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 证明了通过扩展智能体视野，35B模型可以达到或匹配1T模型在长视野任务上的性能，为社区提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: We introduce Agents-A1, a 35B Mixture-of-Experts Agentic Model that reaches trillion-parameter-level performance by scaling the agent horizon. We investigate agent-horizon scaling from two perspectives: scaling long-horizon trajectories and scaling heterogeneous agent abilities. To support this goal, we build a long-horizon knowledge-action infrastructure that connects external knowledge, actions, observations, and verifier outcomes, producing agentic trajectories with an average length of 45K tokens. Based on this, we train Agents-A1 with a three-stage recipe. First, we perform full-domain supervised fine-tuning to align the base model with broad agentic behaviors. Second, we train domain-level teacher models to capture specialized expertise in each domain. Third, we propose a multi-teacher domain-routed on-policy distillation with salient vocabulary alignment to improve knowledge transfer efficiency across different domains, unifying six heterogeneous domains into one deployable student model. Agents-A1 achieves strong and broad performance for long-horizon agent benchmarks. Compared with 1T-parameter model such as Kimi-K2.6 and DeepSeek-V4-pro, Agents-A1 achieves leading results on SEAL-0 (56.4), IFBench (80.6), HiPhO (46.4), FrontierScience-Olympiad (79.0), and MolBench-Bind (56.8), and remains highly competitive on SciCode (44.3), HLE (47.6) and BrowseComp (75.5). We hope this work provides the community with a practical path for scaling the horizon using a 35B agent that can reach or match the performance of 1T models on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [118] [ReFreeKV: Towards Threshold-Free KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16886)
*Xuanfan Ni,Liyan Xu,Chenyang Lyu,Longyue Wang,Mo Yu,Lemao Liu,Fandong Meng,Jie Zhou,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有KV缓存剪枝方法依赖输入特定阈值才能实现无损压缩，这在开放域场景中不现实。本文提出"无阈值"目标，并首次实例化为ReFreeKV方法，在13个数据集上验证了其自适应预算分配的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法需要预先设定输入/领域特定的阈值，这在开放域场景中难以实现，因为输入长度、难度和领域差异巨大，阈值选择不当会导致性能严重下降。

Method: 提出"无阈值"（threshold-free）压缩目标，并实现ReFreeKV方法，自适应调整预算分配，无需依赖预定义阈值即可保持全缓存性能。

Result: 在13个不同上下文长度、任务类型和模型规模的数据集上验证了ReFreeKV的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: ReFreeKV作为首个无阈值KV压缩方法，消除了对输入特定阈值的依赖，更适用于真实开放域场景。

Abstract: To reduce memory consumption during LLM inference, a handful of methods have been proposed for KV cache pruning. While these techniques can accomplish lossless memory reduction on many datasets, they often hinge on an under-emphasized condition: an input/domain-specific threshold for KV cache budget needs to be pre-determined to achieve the optimal performance. However, such input-sensitive design may be considerably limited in real-world scenarios, as open-domain inputs span diverse domains, lengths and difficulty levels, without clear boundaries for threshold selection. As a result, the dependence of such input-sensitive threshold can be a fundamental limitation that causes large degradation on arbitrary inputs. In this work, we propose a new objective that lifts the threshold constraints for robust KV compression, advocating for "threshold-free" methods that adaptively adjust budget allocation while preserving full-cache performance. We then propose a novel method, ReFreeKV, serving as the first instantiation of this objective. Extensive experiments across 13 datasets with diverse context lengths, task types, and model sizes demonstrate its efficacy and efficiency. Our code is publicly released at https://github.com/Patrick-Ni/ReFreeKV.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Recursive Self-Evolving Agents via Held-Out Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28374)
*Michael Nguyen,Quoc Nguyen,Paul Vuong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RSEA（递归自进化智能体），通过携带三层自然语言状态（策略、技能、程序手册）实现无权重更新的智能体进化，并采用严格的保留集门控机制防止性能退化。在四个基准测试中，RSEA在ALFWorld上表现最佳（69.3%），但无门控的上下文进化存在高风险和高方差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体通过进化自然语言工件（如反思、工作流、提示词等）来提升性能，但这些方法通常只在单一基准上报告胜利，缺乏公平一致的横向比较。作者希望系统性地研究这类方法，揭示其真实表现和潜在问题。

Method: 提出RSEA框架，包含三层自然语言状态：指令性策略（strategy）、可复用技能（skills）和程序手册（playbook）。采用递归自进化机制，从自身轨迹重写所有三层，并通过严格的保持更好门控（keep-better gate）——仅在候选版本不在保留集上造成性能退化时才采纳。

Result: 在ALFWorld上RSEA单次方法达69.3%（优于ReAct的64.6%），带重试达79.4%为最佳。但AWM在强骨干工具任务上最优。Dynamic Cheatsheet在ALFWorld上近最佳（70.7%），但在WebShop上崩溃（0.14 vs ReAct的0.43）。RSEA在所有基准上均未显著低于基础智能体。

Conclusion: 没有任何一种工件在所有任务上普遍最优；无保护的上下文进化是高方差且不安全的；RSEA的严格保留集选择机制使递归自进化实现单调安全——它从不显著低于基础智能体，在进化上下文有害时能回退到vanilla ReAct。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly improved without weight updates by evolving a natural-language artifact, such as reflections, workflows, playbooks, cheatsheets, or optimized prompts, that conditions a frozen policy. Such methods are typically reported as wins on the single benchmark where they help. We study them apples-to-apples and surface a sharper picture. We introduce RSEA, a Recursive Self-Evolving Agent that carries a compact three-layer natural-language state: an imperative strategy, reusable skills, and a procedural playbook. Across generations, RSEA rewrites all three layers from its own trajectories and commits a candidate only if it does not regress on a disjoint held-out split, using a strict keep-better gate.
  Across four diverse benchmarks, ALFWorld, GAIA, (τ)-bench, and WebShop, and six faithful baselines, ReAct, Reflexion, GEPA, AWM, ACE, and Dynamic Cheatsheet, all evaluated on one shared local backbone, we find three main results. First, no artifact universally wins. RSEA is the strongest single-pass method on ALFWorld, reaching 69.3% compared with 64.6% for ReAct (McNemar (p=0.015)), and reaches 79.4% with retry, the best overall result. However, concrete-workflow induction, represented by AWM, is best on the strong-backbone tool-use tasks. Second, unguarded context evolution is high-variance and unsafe. Dynamic Cheatsheet, which curates context online without a held-out gate, is near-best on ALFWorld at 70.7%, yet collapses on WebShop, with a score of 0.14 compared with 0.43 for ReAct. Third, RSEA's strict held-out selection is what makes recursive self-evolution monotone-safe: it never significantly underperforms the base agent on any benchmark and falls back to vanilla ReAct when evolved context would hurt.

</details>


### [120] [Data and Evaluation Closed-Loop for Model Capability Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28471)
*Zhixuan Li,Jiangan Yuan,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"能力切片"概念，将评测样本按背景条件、任务类型、求解操作和输出约束分组，构建从评测失败到数据干预的闭环推理框架，并在两个案例中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练中模型能力是核心变量但不可直接观测，评估只能事后给出噪声分数，而优化需要逆向从失败推断语料修复。这两者使用不相容的词汇（基准名/样本正确性 vs 数据源/领域/质量标签），导致推理靠直觉而非方法。

Method: 提出"能力切片"作为评估样本分组单元，结合评估分类法、非指令数据分类法和映射规则，形成从基准级失败到可测试数据干预的闭环。切片区组共享背景条件、任务类型、求解操作和输出约束四个维度。

Result: 案例一：连续预训练导致BBH下降46.82%，诊断发现是<EOS>掩码损失问题而非推理能力下降，修复后BBH恢复至66.44超过原始检查点。案例二：数学推理弱点被分解为具体失败组合，针对性采样使AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128从6.67/0.00提升至26.67。

Conclusion: 该闭环框架在两个方向相反的案例中均得出正确结论，证明评测到数据的推理可以做到常规化、可审计和实验可验证，而非依赖直觉。

Abstract: Model capability is the central variable in LLM pre-training, yet is never observed directly: data shapes it prospectively, while evaluation reveals it only retrospectively, compressing samples, prompts, decoding, and scoring rules into one noisy score. Practical optimization runs this backward: a failure is observed first, and the engineer must infer the corpus fix. The two sides speak incompatible vocabularies -- benchmark names and per-sample correctness versus data sources, domains, and quality labels -- so this inference is usually intuition, not method. We close this gap with the \emph{capability slice}: a group of evaluation samples sharing background condition, task type, solving operation, and output constraint -- precise enough to localize a single weakness yet stable enough to survive aggregation, unlike a benchmark name, too coarse, or a single sample, too noisy. Built around this unit, an evaluation taxonomy, a non-instruction data taxonomy, and mapping rules form a closed loop turning a benchmark-level failure into a targeted, testable data intervention. We test this loop on two case studies pulling in opposite directions. First, the loop rules the data out: continued pre-training drives BBH down by $-46.82\%$, but diagnosis traces this to a single masked \texttt{\textless EOS\textgreater} loss rather than weakened reasoning; restoring it recovers BBH to $66.44$, above the original checkpoint, without changing the data. Second, the loop rules the data in: a persistent math-reasoning weakness is decomposed by solving operation into specific failing combinations, and a weakness-targeted sampling procedure built from it lifts AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128 from $6.67$/$0.00$ to $26.67$ each. The same unmodified loop reaches opposite, correct verdicts in both cases, showing the evaluation-to-data inference can be routine, auditable, and experimentally validated rather than intuitive.

</details>


### [121] [GPTNT: Benchmarking Real-Time Collaboration Between Multimodal Agents on Keep Talking And Nobody Explodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28514)
*Amit Parekh,Sabrina McCallum,Kareem Al-Hasan,Malvina Nikandrou,Alessandro Suglia,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GPTNT基准测试，基于合作游戏《Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes》，评估多模态模型在时间压力、信息不对称和实时通信条件下的协作能力。结果显示当前最先进模型均无法在实时条件下拆除任何炸弹，而人类玩家可以轻松完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常在隔离条件下研究时间压力、信息不对称和不完美通信等协作要素，缺乏将这些条件综合在一起的评估，因此需要一个新的基准来衡量多模态模型在真实协作场景中的表现。

Method: 基于合作视频游戏《Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes》构建GPTNT基准。两名智能体需要实时异步协作：一个能看见和操作炸弹但没有拆弹说明，另一个有说明但看不见炸弹。通过选择性保留说明手册、合作伙伴等来隔离分析模型从即时交互中获得的信息与已有知识。

Result: 测试的所有闭源和开源模型在实时条件下都无法拆除任何一颗炸弹，而人类玩家可以完成。模型在状态追踪、时间压力下的有效行动、歧义处理和错误恢复方面存在严重缺陷。

Conclusion: GPTNT作为一个可演化、可持续的协作性能基准测试，能够补充现有评估体系的空白。由于基于真实游戏并受益于程序生成和活跃的模组社区，该基准可以随模型进步而发展，不会一次性被解决后退役。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly deployed to solve tasks collaboratively with humans or other artificial agents. Existing benchmarks show that these models possess many of the required component capabilities, but the conditions that coincide in collaboration, including time pressure, information asymmetry, and imperfect communication, are usually studied in isolation. We introduce GPTNT, a benchmark built on the cooperative video game Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes, in which two agents must coordinate to defuse procedurally generated bomb puzzles against a live countdown. One agent can see and manipulate the bomb but does not have the defusal instructions; the other has the instructions but cannot see or manipulate the bomb. Neither agent can succeed alone: success requires effective and efficient communication. Unlike turn-based proxies, GPTNT requires agents to act asynchronously and communicate in real time. GPTNT is designed to separate collaboration from reliance on memorized solutions: the instruction manual, the partner, or both can be withheld to isolate what a model derives in the moment from what it already knows. We show that GPTNT poses a substantial challenge for state-of-the-art systems: none of the closed- or open-source models we test defuses a single bomb in real time, a bar that human players clear. Through controlled experiments, we identify critical weaknesses in state tracking, efficient action under time pressure, ambiguity handling, and error recovery. We release GPTNT as a benchmark for collaborative performance that current evaluations leave unmeasured. Because it runs on the real game, GPTNT benefits from procedural generation and inherits a living modding community, allowing the benchmark to evolve as models improve rather than being solved once and retired.

</details>


### [122] [IMCBench: A benchmark for multimodal LLMs in Image-grounded Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28556)
*Maria Xenochristou,Ashutosh Joshi,Korosh Vatanparvar,Mohammad Abuzar Hashemi,Prasad Kasu,Deepak Bansal,Anchal Nema,Nivedita Wadhwa,Prashams S Jain,Rebecca Abraham,Will Kimbrough,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Wilko Schulz-Mahlendorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IMCBench——一个结合真实临床图像与合成患者档案的多轮医学对话基准，评估8个多模态前沿模型在安全性、准确性和不确定性表达三个维度上的表现。结果显示Claude Opus 4.6综合得分最高（3.61），但无模型在所有维度上占优，且恶性与罕见疾病场景下安全性下降，视觉与EHR信息均对安全引导有重要贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI基准存在碎片化问题：有的支持多轮对话但缺乏图像，有的提供多模态输入但仅限单轮问答，缺乏同时结合图像与多轮对话的综合评估基准。

Method: 构建IMCBench基准，将真实公开临床图像与合成患者档案配对，模拟多轮医患对话；采用LLM-as-Jury评分（1-5分），经临床专家标注校准，从安全性、准确性、不确定性表达三个维度评估8个多模态前沿模型（Claude、GPT、Nova、Llama四大家族）。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6综合得分最高（3.61），Claude Sonnet 4.6（3.30）和GPT-5.2（3.29）次之；恶性与罕见疾病场景安全性下降（Δ=-0.27）；消融实验显示移除视觉输入或EHR上下文分别导致安全评分下降0.18和0.23。

Conclusion: 准确的临床描述并不能保证安全的患者引导，医学AI评估需要涵盖安全性、准确性和不确定性表达的多维度框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and vision-language models have enabled reasoning over multimodal data, offering opportunities for clinical applications such as decision support and triaging. However, existing medical AI benchmarks are fragmented: some support multi-turn dialogues but lack images, while others provide multimodal inputs but focus on single-turn QA tasks. To address this gap, we introduce IMCBench, an image-grounded, multi-turn medical conversation benchmark that pairs real, publicly available clinical images with synthetic patient profiles to simulate realistic patient-clinician interactions. Each conversation is evaluated across three clinical dimensions: safety, accuracy, and appropriate use of uncertainty in diagnosis. We benchmark eight multimodal frontier models across four model families (Claude, GPT, Nova, and Llama), scoring each on a 1-5 scale using LLM-as-Jury scoring calibrated against expert clinician annotations. Our results show that Claude Opus 4.6 achieves the highest overall score (3.61), followed by Claude Sonnet 4.6 (3.30) and GPT-5.2 (3.29), though no model dominates all dimensions and safety degrades for both malignant and rare conditions ($Δ$ = -0.27 each). Ablation studies further reveal that both visual input and EHR context contribute to safe guidance (safety drops of 0.18 and 0.23 on average when each is removed), with stronger models leveraging visual features more effectively. Together, these findings demonstrate that accurate clinical description does not guarantee safe patient guidance, motivating the need for multi-dimensional evaluation frameworks in medical AI.

</details>


### [123] [Search for Truth from Reasoning: A Dynamic Representation Editing Framework for Steering LLM Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28589)
*Tianlong Wang,Yuhang Wang,Weibin Liao,Xin Gao,Xinyu Ma,Yang Lin,Yasha Wang,Liantao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DynaSteer框架，通过对推理链中的真实性几何结构进行分析，利用模式聚类和Fisher-LDA投影，在早期高熵分支点动态引导LLM推理，提升数学和代码推理的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如CoT、"Wait"提示）虽鼓励模型多思考，但未能有效引导模型走向真实；表示编辑（RepE）虽提供内在控制，但在动态推理轨迹中的应用尚未充分探索。

Method: 1. 揭示真实性在句子级别编码且与潜在推理模式纠缠；2. 发现干预需遵循"不确定性原理"和"衰减效应"，定位到早期高熵分支点；3. 提出DynaSteer框架：利用模式聚类解耦推理流形，Fisher-LDA投影提纯真实性向量，结合动态前瞻熵选择性引导和回滚轨迹。

Result: 在多个MATH基准上验证有效性，在域外编程任务上进一步验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: DynaSteer通过动态、选择性的真实性引导，在不破坏正确推理路径的前提下，显著提升了LLM推理的真实性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Current approaches to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, such as Chain-of-Thought and "Wait" prompts, primarily encourage models to think more, yet often fail to guide them toward Truth. While Representation Editing (RepE) offers a intrinsic control, its application to dynamic reasoning trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we bridge this gap by investigating the geometry of truth within unfolding reasoning chains. We uncover three critical insights: (1) Truth is encoded at the sentence level and is entangled with latent reasoning patterns; (2) Effective intervention follows an Uncertainty Principle and a Decay Effect, requiring localization to early, high-entropy forks; (3) Naive steering vectors suffer from noise, risking collateral damage to correct trajectories. Based on these findings, we propose DynaSteer, a dynamic RepE framework. DynaSteer employs pattern clustering to disentangle reasoning manifolds and utilizes Fisher-LDA to project purified truth. By dynamically monitoring lookahead entropy, it selectively steers and rolls back trajectories only when necessary. Comprehensive experimental results on several MATH benchmark verify the effectiveness of DynaSteer, and experiments on out-of-domain coding tasks further confirm its generalization ability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tianlwang/DynaSteer.

</details>


### [124] [Aristotelian Virtue Profiling of LLMs through Ethical Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28683)
*Ioannis Tzachristas,John Pavlopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VirtueMap框架，基于亚里士多德美德伦理学评估LLM在伦理困境中的决策倾向，通过排序评分和标准化Borda对齐计算美德画像，发现LLM在勇气、节制和正义维度差异最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM面对伦理困境时常存在多种合理但侧重不同美德的回答，现有方法缺乏系统描述这些伦理权衡模式的框架。

Method: 设计7个非敏感伦理困境，每个困境5个回答；针对每个美德提出回答排序并收集至少100份人类确认（95%一致性作为标准）；用标准化Borda对齐计算美德画像；对9个LLM家族进行重复评估。

Result: LLM平均排名一致性达90.3%，最大差异出现在勇气、节制和正义维度；发布了交互式网站用于本地计算和对比。

Conclusion: VirtueMap能有效刻画LLM的伦理权衡模式，揭示不同模型在美德维度上的差异化表现。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often face ethical tradeoffs in which several responses may be defensible but express different priorities, such as fairness, honesty, courage, or restraint. We introduce VirtueMap, a framework for describing these patterns through an Aristotelian virtue-ethics lens. Instead of asking for a single correct answer, VirtueMap asks humans or LLMs to rank all five responses to each of seven general, non-lethal, non-political, and non-religious ethical dilemmas. To define the reference orderings used for scoring, we first proposed, for each dilemma and virtue, an ordering of the five responses from most to least expressive of that virtue. We then collected more than 100 respondent evaluations per ordering and retained it as operational ground truth only when at least 95% confirmed it. Rankings are scored against these retained orderings using normalized Borda alignment, yielding profiles over Practical Wisdom, Justice, Truthfulness, Courage, and Temperance. We apply VirtueMap to nine LLM families in a repeated-run evaluation and find high mean rank consistency (90.3%), with the largest differences appearing on Courage, Temperance, and Justice. We also release an interactive website that computes profiles locally in the browser and compares respondents with measured LLM profiles.

</details>


### [125] [An AI agent for treatment reasoning over a biomedical tool universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28692)
*Shanghua Gao,Ayush Noori,Richard Zhu,Curtis Ginder,Zhenglun Kong,Xiaorui Su,Justin Kauffman,Benjamin S. Glicksberg,Joshua Lampert,Ankit Sakhuja,Ashwin Sawant,ATHENA-R1 Evaluation Consortium,David A. Clifton,Noa Dagan,Ran Balicer,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ATHENA-R1，一个基于强化学习的AI治疗推理智能体，覆盖1939年以来所有FDA批准药物。通过双层自学习框架（多智能体构建+强化学习科学反馈）训练，无需人工标注，在5个基准测试中显著优于GPT-5等模型，并在罕见病专家和医生评估中表现优异。治疗推理被重构为可学习的迭代证据收集过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗推理涉及疾病背景、合并症、药物、禁忌症等多重约束的迭代权衡，传统AI难以处理。现有方法缺乏系统性证据收集能力，且依赖人工标注数据。作者希望构建一个无需人工标注、能自主学习证据收集的AI智能体来解决这一难题。

Method: 采用双层自学习框架：(1) 上层：多智能体系统构建工具、任务和推理轨迹，用于监督微调；(2) 下层：强化学习结合科学反馈（证据收集、工具使用、逻辑非冗余性）训练模型。模型在每一步识别缺失信息、选择并运行相关工具、整合证据。

Result: 在3,168个药物推理任务和456个患者治疗案例上，ATHENA-R1在开放药物推理达94.7%准确率，治疗推理达82.9%，分别超GPT-5 17.8和10.7个百分点。来自28个罕见病组织的专家盲评中全面优于参考模型。生成的药物不良反应假设在540万患者EHR数据中验证，调整后比值比达1.48-1.84，阴性对照无异常。

Conclusion: 治疗推理这一长期困扰AI的难题可以重构为可学习的迭代证据收集过程，强化学习能够有效训练AI执行此类推理。ATHENA-R1证明了无需人工标注即可训练出高质量的治疗推理智能体。

Abstract: Treatment reasoning underpins every therapeutic decision, integrating disease context, comorbidities, medications, contraindications, and evolving biomedical knowledge to select an appropriate therapy. It is inherently iterative: candidates are weighed against many constraints, revised as evidence emerges, and grounded in verifiable sources. Here we introduce ATHENA-R1, an AI agent for treatment reasoning across all FDA approved drugs since 1939, trained by reinforcement learning over a universe of 212 biomedical tools. At each step it identifies missing information, selects and runs relevant tools, and incorporates the evidence. To train it without human-annotated traces, we build a two-level self-learning framework: multi-agent systems construct the tools, tasks, and reasoning trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, then reinforcement learning with scientific feedback rewards reasoning quality (evidence gathering, grounded tool use, logical non-redundancy). Across five benchmarks of 3,168 drug reasoning tasks and 456 patient treatment cases, ATHENA-R1 outperforms language models and tool-use systems, reaching 94.7% accuracy on open-ended drug reasoning and 82.9% on treatment reasoning, 17.8 and 10.7 points above GPT-5. In blinded evaluations by experts from 28 rare disease organizations, it is preferred over reference models on all criteria, and physicians rated it favorably on complex hospitalized cardiovascular and infectious-disease cases. Adverse-event hypotheses it generated, tested in electronic health records from 5.4 million patients, reached adjusted odds ratios of 1.48-1.84, with no elevation among negative controls. Because it requires knowing what evidence to seek before concluding, treatment reasoning has long been hard for AI; we show it can be reframed as a learnable process of iterative evidence gathering that reinforcement learning can train AI to perform.

</details>


### [126] [COMPASS: Grounding Composition-Intent Guidance in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28696)
*Ziqi Zhou,Weize Quan,Mining Tan,Zhihan Chen,Dandan Zheng,Jingdong Chen,Jun Zhou,Weiming Dong,Dong-Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMPASS是首个统一的多模态框架，通过共享专家令牌τ_c实现构图感知与构图引导生成，在MoE骨干网络上注入构图专业知识，显著提升了构图理解与可控生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在细粒度构图识别上不可靠，且难以将构图意图转化为可控生成，缺乏一个统一的系统来同时处理构图感知和构图引导生成。

Method: 提出COMPASS框架：1)在MoE骨干上以最小侵入方式注入构图专家知识，将推理意图蒸馏到共享令牌τ_c；2)在生成侧复用τ_c作为全局条件信号引导去噪轨迹；3)构建Comp-11大规模数据集（11类分类体系+推理增强注释）。

Result: 大幅提升类别级构图理解能力，生成结果相比强基线具有更好的构图一致性和提示忠实度。

Conclusion: COMPASS通过统一的共享专家令牌机制，成功将构图感知与构图引导生成整合在单一系统中，验证了构图意图锚点在多模态理解与生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Composition is a high-level visual intent that governs where subjects are placed and how a scene is organized, yet current unified multimodal models remain unreliable at fine-grained composition recognition and struggle to turn such intent into controllable generation. We present COMPASS, the first unified multimodal framework that grounds composition-intent control in a single system spanning both composition perception and composition-guided generation, with a shared expert token $τ_c$ as the central intent anchor. On the perception side, COMPASS injects composition expertise into an MoE backbone in a minimally invasive manner and distills the inferred intent into $τ_c$. On the generation side, COMPASS reuses $τ_c$ as a global conditioning signal that steers the denoising trajectory, effectively converting passive composition analysis into explicit layout control. To support systematic instruction-following composition learning and evaluation at scale, we construct Comp-11, a large-scale dataset with an 11-class taxonomy and reasoning-augmented annotations. Extensive experiments show that COMPASS substantially improves category-level composition understanding and delivers more composition-consistent, prompt-faithful generation than strong baselines.

</details>


### [127] [BV-Blend: Uncertainty-Weighted Historical Baselines for Stable Critic-Free RL with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28707)
*Yupeng Chang,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BV-Blend是一种无需评论家的强化学习框架，通过结合语义簇条件历史矩与提示局部在线统计来稳定优势估计，解决了GRPO在奖励方差为零时学习停滞的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等无评论家RLVR方法虽节省内存和计算，但其依赖提示局部奖励统计的优势估计不稳定，当同组所有rollout获得相同奖励时方差为零、优势为零，导致冷启动阶段学习停滞。

Method: BV-Blend维护每个语义簇的EMA追踪奖励矩，通过标准误代理导出置信权重，用该权重混合历史与提示局部的基线和方差统计，生成标准化优势用于PPO风格的裁剪更新。

Result: 在可验证推理基准上，BV-Blind提升了训练稳定性和性能，在组归一化方法可能停滞的设定下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: BV-Blend有效解决了无评论家RLVR中优势估计不稳定的问题，为二元验证器下的冷启动学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), exemplified by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), avoids training a value function (critic) and reduces memory and compute overhead relative to critic-based PPO pipelines for aligning large language models. However, GRPO-style advantage estimation depends on prompt-local (within-prompt-group) reward statistics and can be unstable. In particular, when all rollouts in a prompt group receive identical rewards, the within-group reward variance becomes zero, and group normalization yields zero advantages for that group, impeding learning in cold-start regimes with binary verifiers. We introduce BV-Blend, a critic-free framework that stabilizes advantage estimation by combining prompt-local on-policy statistics with semantic-cluster-conditioned historical moments. BV-Blend maintains EMA-tracked reward moments for each cluster, derives a confidence weight from a standard error of the mean (SEM) proxy, and uses this weight to blend historical and prompt-local baseline and variance statistics into a standardized advantage for PPO-style clipped updates. Experiments on verifiable reasoning benchmarks show that BV-Blend improves training stability and performance, and remains robust in regimes where group-normalized methods may stall.

</details>


### [128] [The Two Genie Game: Adoption and Welfare in Audit-Grounded AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28710)
*Darrell Lewis-Sandy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用演化博弈论研究在负和竞争市场中，采用最小化伤害策略的智能体能否取代基于RLHF（追求认可）的智能体，并分析该策略是否足以防止群体伤害。研究表明，在某些先验分布条件下，该策略更易被采纳，但即便采纳，也不能保证防止群体伤害——是否有效取决于审计与社区价值观的契合度以及伤害评估的时间跨度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在竞争市场中，采用最小化伤害策略的智能体能否取代追求认可的RLHF智能体，以及该策略是否足以防止社区伤害。

Method: 使用有限种群Moran-Fermi成对比较的演化博弈论进行形式化建模，并在Lean 4中进行了机器验证。

Result: 当先验分布满足单调性、端点反转和中心对称配对性质时，最小化伤害策略更易被采纳；存在临界采纳水平，超过该水平后智能体固定成为压倒性可能的结果；社区有效信息规模N_c需足够小才能实现固定。

Conclusion: 自我审计的智能体配合社区账本通常不足以防止社区伤害，其有效性取决于审计与社区价值观的对齐程度以及伤害评估的时间框架。一旦采纳达到主导地位，该状态即被锁定。

Abstract: We ask under what conditions an agent with a harm-minimizing policy can displace an approval-seeking (RLHF) agent in a competitive market, and when that policy is sufficient to prevent community harm. We use evolutionary game theory (finite-population Moran-Fermi pairwise comparison) to formalize this subject to assumptions of wisher hindsight, peer testimony, a monotone harm ledger, sufficient information density of community feedback, and a finite, depleting resource pool, in a negative-sum environment.
  We show that adoption is favored when the prior distributions on how readily wishers attune to community sentiment are monotone, exhibit endpoint inversion, and have a centro-symmetric pairing property, and demonstrate this with several long-tailed priors (Hill, Pareto, Lomax, Frechet). Where it is favored, a critical adoption level separates communities that drift back to the approval-seeking agent from those for which the audited agent fixes; above that level fixation is the overwhelmingly likely outcome. We derive when fixation is attainable as a bound on the effective (informational) size N_c of the community, which must be small enough to allow fixation before depletion. We present these as Theorems 5.4 and 5.5; the algebraic and finite-grid backbone is machine-checked in Lean 4, with the barrier-crossing asymptotics retained as explicit hypotheses.
  We show that a self-audited agent with a community ledger is not, in general, sufficient to prevent community harm. Sufficiency depends both upon the alignment of the agent's audit with community values and the timeframe over which harm is evaluated. Regardless of alignment, once adoption reaches dominance, the state is absorbing. The same policy that reduced harm under alignment becomes a trap, welfare-negative under misalignment and, even under alignment, one that locks in harm deferred past the adoption horizon.

</details>


### [129] [TrajRS: Towards Certified Robustness in Pedestrian Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28716)
*Liang Zhang,Gaojie Jin,Yao Shi,Quanzhi Li,Cheng-Chao Huang,David N. Jansen,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajRS框架，将随机平滑扩展到轨迹预测领域，为平滑后的轨迹预测器提供可验证的鲁棒半径，并针对"最优预测鲁棒性"和"所有可能预测鲁棒性"两种定义进行形式化扩展与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹预测模型的鲁棒性对自动驾驶安全至关重要，现有启发式防御策略难以应对复杂对抗攻击，亟需建立可验证的安全保证。

Method: 将传统随机平滑框架扩展为TrajRS，形式化定义轨迹预测中的鲁棒性概念，并针对最优预测和所有可能预测两种鲁棒性场景设计具体方案。

Result: 实验表明TrajRS能够有效为所有平滑后的行人轨迹预测器提供鲁棒性认证。

Conclusion: TrajRS为轨迹预测模型提供了可验证的鲁棒性保证，弥补了启发式防御的不足，提升了自动驾驶系统的安全性。

Abstract: The robustness of trajectory prediction models is crucial for developing safe autonomous driving systems. Adversarial attacks on trajectory prediction can significantly impair the accuracy of predicted trajectories, leading to hazardous driving behaviors. While heuristic defense strategies have been implemented to enhance the robustness of trajectory prediction models, these measures often fail against more sophisticated, targeted adversarial attacks. Hence, there is a pressing need to establish verifiable safety assurances for trajectory prediction models. In this paper, we extend the traditional Randomized Smoothing framework to "TrajRS", which provides a certified robust radius for smoothed trajectory predictors. We clarify and expand the formal definitions of robustness in trajectory prediction and tailor the practical TrajRS scheme specifically to "robustness for the optimal prediction" and "robustness for all possible predictions". An extensive set of experiments demonstrates that TrajRS effectively achieves robustness certification for all smoothed pedestrian trajectory predictors in this work.

</details>


### [130] [ComMem: Complementary Memory Systems for Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28719)
*Guanglong Sun,Shuang Cui,Bo Lei,Liyuan Wang,Zihan Zhai,Hongwei Yan,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ComMem是一种受生物大脑互补记忆系统启发的测试时自适应方法，通过快速适应的详细记忆（类似海马体）和慢速整合的抽象记忆（类似新皮层）协同工作，显著提升了视觉语言模型在动态环境中的鲁棒性，在15个基准数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法要么局部适应而不积累知识，要么在单一模态内操作，未能充分利用视觉语言模型的多模态特性。受生物大脑互补记忆系统的启发，旨在解决这一问题。

Method: ComMem包含两个关键组件：1) 快速适应的详细记忆（类似海马体），从高置信度测试样本中构建动态视觉缓存；2) 慢速整合的抽象记忆（类似新皮层），持续精炼全局文本原型。对每个测试样本，联合优化两个记忆系统以确保跨模态一致性。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，ComMem在自然分布偏移和跨数据集泛化场景下均显著优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: ComMem为增强视觉语言模型的实际适应性提供了一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) of vision-language models (VLMs) is essential for their robust deployment in dynamic, real-world environments. However, existing TTA methods often adapt locally without accumulating knowledge over time, or operating within a single modality without exploiting VLMs' inherently multi-modal nature. Inspired by the \textbf{Com}plementary \textbf{Mem}ory systems of the biological brain, we propose \textbf{ComMem}, an innovative approach that mimics the distinct but cooperative roles of the hippocampus and neocortex to enable effective TTA for VLMs. ComMem consists of two key components: a fast-adapting detailed memory, akin to the hippocampus, that forms a dynamic visual cache from high-confidence test samples; and a slow-integrating abstract memory, akin to the neocortex, that continually refines global textual prototypes. For each test instance, ComMem jointly optimizes both memory systems to ensure cross-modal consistency. Extensive experiments on 15 benchmark datasets show that ComMem significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods under both natural distribution shifts and cross-dataset generalization, offering a promising direction for enhancing VLMs' practical adaptability.

</details>


### [131] [Agentic Abstention: Do Agents Know When to Stop Instead of Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28733)
*Han Luo,Bingbing Wen,Lucy Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了"智能体回避"问题——LLM智能体在不确定时应停止行动，而非盲目调用工具。研究涉及网页购物、终端环境和问答三大场景，测试了13个LLM智能体和2种脚手架，发现主要挑战不仅是能否回避，更是何时回避。提出的CONVOLVE方法通过将完整交互轨迹蒸馏为可复用的停止规则，有效提升了及时回避率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM回避研究多聚焦于单轮问答中的"答或弃"决策，但实际智能体需要在多轮交互中判断是否应停止行动。当任务目标不明确或环境无法实现时，可靠智能体应能识别这一点并主动停止，而非无效调用工具。目前缺乏对这一"智能体回避"问题的系统性研究。

Method: 定义了Agentic Abstention问题，在网页购物、终端环境和问答三个场景中，对13个LLM智能体系统和2种智能体脚手架进行超过28,000个任务的评估。提出CONVOLVE方法，这是一种上下文工程技术，将完整的交互轨迹蒸馏为可复用的停止规则，无需更新模型参数。

Result: 主要挑战不仅是是否回避，更是何时回避。有些智能体从未在应该回避时回避，有些则在大量无效交互后才回避。更大或更强模型在及时回避方面有时表现更差。CONVOLVE方法在WebShop上将Llama-3.3-70B的及时召回率从26.7提升至57.4。

Conclusion: 智能体回避是一个重要的顺序决策问题，现有智能体在"何时回避"上存在显著差距。CONVOLVE方法无需修改模型参数即可有效提升及时回避能力，为构建更可靠的LLM智能体提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM agents are expected to act over multiple turns, using search, browsing interfaces, and terminal tools to complete user goals. Yet not every goal is well specified or achievable in the available environment. In such cases, a reliable agent should recognize that further interaction is unlikely to help and abstain from additional tool calls. We define Agentic Abstention, the problem of deciding when an agent should stop acting under uncertainty. Unlike standard LLM abstention, which is usually evaluated as a single-turn answer-or-abstain decision, agentic abstention is a sequential decision problem: an agent can answer, abstain, or gather more information at each turn, and the need to abstain may only become clear after interacting with the environment. We study this problem across web shopping, terminal environments, and question answering, evaluating 13 LLM-as-agent systems and 2 agent scaffolds on more than 28,000 tasks. Our results show that the main challenge is not only whether agents can abstain, but also when they abstain. Some agents never abstain when they should, while others do so only after many unnecessary interactions. This gap is especially large on tasks where the instruction appears feasible until the environment reveals otherwise (e.g., no valid result matches the instruction). We further find that model scale, reasoning, and agent scaffolding affect abstention in different ways, where larger or more capable models sometimes perform worse at timely abstention. Finally, we introduce CONVOLVE, a context engineering method for improving agentic abstention that distills full interaction trajectories into reusable stopping rules. On WebShop, CONVOLVE substantially improves timely abstention without updating model parameters, raising Llama-3.3-70B's timely recall rate from 26.7 to 57.4. Our dataset and code are available at https://lhannnn.github.io/agentic-abstention

</details>


### [132] [Agent Safety Is Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28739)
*Shawn Li,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证将聊天机器人的内容安全拒绝机制直接套用到智能体场景是概念错误：智能体的伤害不在于输出内容，而在于行动与授权之间的不对等关系。通过三条证据表明，这种移植不仅损失能力，还会带来负面安全效果，并提出行动安全应通过最小权限原则在模型外部强制执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前实践将大语言模型作为智能体使用时，错误地沿用了聊天机器人时代的\"拒绝不安全输入\"的安全范式，将能力损失视为可接受的\"对齐税\"。作者指出这是一种\"范畴错误\"：内容安全与行动安全有本质区别，前者伤害存在于模型输出中，后者伤害在于行动与用户授权之间的关系。

Method: 作者通过三条证据线进行论证：1) 防御训练的模型学习表面模式而非意图；2) 同样的训练会破坏多步智能体能力，同时仍存在可被利用的漏洞；3) 未防御的顶级模型在常规使用中也会超越被授予的权限。

Result: 研究表明，将内容安全方法移植到智能体领域，不是用能力换取安全，而是付出了能力成本却买来了负面安全效果。也就是说，这种安全策略既损害了模型功能，又未能提供真正的安全保障。

Conclusion: 行动安全无法被安装到模型权重中。必须通过最小权限原则，在模型外部的行动边界强制执行，并作为\"行动对齐\"（一种关系性的、依赖部署条件的属性）进行评估，而非仅仅依赖拒绝评分。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly act as agents: they call tools, move money, delete records, and send messages on a user's behalf. To keep them safe, practitioners imported the chatbot-era recipe (train the model to refuse unsafe inputs) into the agentic setting, and treat the resulting capability loss as a manageable ``alignment tax.'' We argue this is a \emph{category error}. Refusal is a primitive for \emph{content safety}, where the harm is in the model's output and is therefore a learnable function of it. Agentic harm is different in kind: it lies not in any output but in the relation between the authority an action exercises and the authority the user granted, which is absent from the text the model sees. Importing content-safety methods into this regime does not trade capability for safety; it pays capability and buys negative security. We support this with three lines of evidence spanning the autonomy spectrum: defense-trained models learn surface patterns rather than intent; the same training collapses multi-step agents before any threat appears while leaving them exploitable; and even undefended frontier models exceed granted authority under ordinary use. We conclude that action safety cannot be installed in weights. It must be expressed as \emph{least privilege}, enforced \emph{outside} the model at the action boundary, and evaluated as \emph{action alignment} (a relational, deployment-conditioned property) rather than a refusal score.

</details>


### [133] [Self-Supervised Theorem Discovery in a Formal Axiomatic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28747)
*Kazuki Ota,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需人类先验知识的自监督定理发现算法，该算法通过交替进行证明搜索和有用定理提取，自主构建定理库。实验表明该算法能发现数万条定理，可解决人类编写的基准问题，并能作为外部知识提升大语言模型的证明性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统能否在无需人类先验知识（如数学文本、代码或定理库）的条件下，在形式化公理系统中自主发现有用定理。

Method: 提出一种自监督定理发现算法，在形式化公理系统中从公理和推理规则出发，交替执行证明搜索和有用定理提取，逐步构建可复用的定理库，并将库中条目作为后续证明搜索的引理。

Result: 该算法发现了数万条定理，能够解决人类编写的基准问题；同时，发现的定理作为提示引理可提升大语言模型的证明性能。

Conclusion: 有用定理可以从证明搜索中涌现，无需依赖人类提供的定理库；这为构建可自我演进的、结果形式化可验证的数学AI系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent artificial intelligence (AI) systems have shown remarkable progress in mathematical reasoning. Many existing approaches, including large language models (LLMs), draw on human prior knowledge in the form of mathematical text, code, or theorem libraries. Although these approaches are highly effective in practice, it remains an open question whether an agent can autonomously discover useful theorems without such human priors. We study this question in a formal axiomatic system by developing an agent that starts from axioms and inference rules alone and gradually grows a library of useful theorems. Concretely, we propose a self-supervised theorem-discovery algorithm that alternates between proof search and useful-theorem extraction, building a theorem library whose entries are reused as lemmas for subsequent proof search. Experiments show that the agent discovers tens of thousands of theorems and finds proofs for human-written benchmark problems, suggesting that its discoveries include theorems meaningful from a human mathematical perspective. Furthermore, the discovered theorems improve LLM proof performance when provided as prompt lemmas, indicating that they can serve as external knowledge for LLM reasoning. Our results provide evidence that useful theorems can emerge from proof search without relying on human-provided theorem libraries. More broadly, they suggest a path toward self-evolving AI systems for mathematics whose discoveries remain formally verifiable.

</details>


### [134] [Mechanistic Personality Analysis of LLMs Steering Personality via Latent Feature Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28770)
*David Courtis,Ting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于稀疏自编码器和对比激活分析的机械可解释性方法，通过在残差流中识别并干预与大五人格特质相关的潜在方向，实现对LLM生成文本人格特质的可控调节，同时保持语言建模性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖提示工程或微调来塑造LLM人格，缺乏对模型内部机制的深入理解和可控干预，需要一种能从机械层面直接操控人格特质的方法。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取残差流中的潜在特征，结合对比激活分析识别与目标OCEAN特质对应的潜在方向；设计加性引导向量对隐藏状态进行微小平移；采用线性加权启发式与网格搜索优化特征偏移组合，平衡人格表达与任务性能。

Result: 该方法能在机械层面可控地引导人格特质，同时保持标准基准测试上的高性能表现。

Conclusion: 基于机械可解释性的潜特征干预为人格特质操控提供了比提示工程和微调更可控、更精细的新范式，在保持模型能力的同时实现人格层面的定向调节。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated the ability to simulate human-like OCEAN personality traits in generated text. Previous efforts have focused on prompt engineering or fine-tuning to shape LLM personality. In this work, we propose a mechanistic interpretability approach that directly intervenes on the model's latent features. Our method identifies latent directions in the residual stream corresponding to a target OCEAN trait using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) and contrastive activation analysis. We formalize an additive steering vector in activation space and demonstrate how applying a small additive shift to the hidden states enhances the target trait while preserving overall language modeling performance. To determine the optimal combination of feature shifts, we explore a linear weighting heuristic with grid search optimization that balances personality expression with task performance. Our approach shows promise in controllably steering personality traits at the mechanistic level while maintaining high performance on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [135] [HyphaeDB: A Living Knowledge Topology for Agent-First Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28781)
*Krishna Halaharvi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyphaeDB 是一个基于 HNSW 图拓扑结构的智能体原生记忆基础设施，通过 gossip 协议实现多智能体间的知识传播与涌现行为，首次将导航小世界拓扑与知识传播相结合用于多智能体协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量数据库和智能体记忆框架都将记忆视为被动存储，需要智能体显式查询，没有系统能通过记忆层本身在智能体之间传播知识。

Method: 基于三个原语构建：知识节点、拓扑边和记忆差异；采用多层级抽象体系结构，通过涌现共识实现知识提升；结合小世界网络理论、流行病广播协议和群体智能进行理论分析；在 PostgreSQL 上基于 pgvector 实现参考实现。

Result: 提出了 HyphaeDB 架构，包含三原语设计、多层级抽象层次结构，以及基于小世界网络理论、流行病广播协议和群体智能的理论分析；提供了基于 PostgreSQL/pgvector 的参考实现和 Swarm-Driven Development 的部署案例。

Conclusion: HyphaeDB 是首个将导航小世界拓扑与基于 gossip 的知识传播相结合用于多智能体协调的系统，能够实现矛盾检测、模式结晶和共识形成等涌现行为。

Abstract: Every existing vector database and agent memory framework treats memory as passive storage that agents query explicitly. No system propagates knowledge between agents through the memory layer itself. We introduce HyphaeDB, an agent-native memory infrastructure that reinterprets the Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) graph topology the data structure at the core of every modern vector database not as a search optimization, but as a communication fabric for multi-agent AI systems. In HyphaeDB, agents are nodes in the vector space with persistent positions, knowledge propagates via a gossip protocol through the graph's neighbor structure with energy-based attenuation, and emergent behaviors contradiction detection, pattern crystallization, and consensus formation arise from the combination of topology, propagation dynamics, and local interaction rules. We present the architecture built on three primitives (knowledge nodes, topology edges, and memory diffs), a multi-layer abstraction hierarchy with promotion via emergent consensus, and theoretical analysis grounding the system in small-world network theory, epidemic broadcast protocols, and swarm intelligence. We provide a reference implementation on PostgreSQL with pgvector and describe a concrete deployment in Swarm-Driven Development, a multi-agent software engineering methodology. HyphaeDB represents, to our knowledge, the first system to combine navigable small world topology with gossip-based knowledge propagation for multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [136] [Primary ICD Category Prediction using LLM-based Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28798)
*Chengyuan Liu,Xinyue Zhang,Yao Li,Guanting Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用冻结的医学大语言模型（如MedFound-Llama3-8B）作为共享嵌入空间，通过线性探针融合结构化数据和非结构化临床文本，进行多模态主要诊断类别预测。在MIMIC-IV数据集上，联合探针达到87.69%的严格准确率和91.45%的医学准确率，且仅用2M参数的小型适配器即可实现跨数据集迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动编码系统难以有效整合来自临床叙述和结构化电子健康记录（EHR）变量的诊断信号，而ICD编码对报销、研究和人群健康监测至关重要。作者希望探索能否利用冻结的大语言模型表示作为统一的共享嵌入空间，实现多模态诊断预测。

Method: 构建MIMIC-IV队列（13,645次入院，涵盖10个最常见ICD-10代码，合并为7个类别）；将结构化变量序列化为临床叙述，与脱敏出院笔记结合；使用冻结的MedFound-Llama3-8B微调骨干网络，提取五个Transformer层的隐藏状态；训练线性探针处理结构化、非结构化及组合输入；与XGBoost和PLM-ICD基线对比；使用紧凑型bottleneck适配器评估MIMIC-III跨数据集迁移。

Result: 联合探针在MIMIC-IV上表现最佳（严格准确率87.69%，医学准确率91.45%），优于单模态探针和基线；结构化探针的医学准确率比标准基线高出6.19个百分点；诊断信息在更深层中线性可分性增强；仅用2M参数的适配器和5%目标标签即可恢复MIMIC-III的跨数据集迁移性能。

Conclusion: 对冻结的医学大语言模型进行多模态探针研究，为研究EHR模态和跨数据集适应临床表示提供了一种实用方法。LLM嵌入能够统一结构化和叙事型EHR信息，通过小型表示级模块实现高效的跨模态和跨数据集临床表示复用。

Abstract: Objective: ICD codes are central to reimbursement, research, and population health surveillance, yet automated coding systems often struggle to integrate diagnostic signals from both clinical narratives and structured electronic health record (EHR) variables. We evaluated whether frozen medical large language model (LLM) representations can serve as a shared embedding space for multimodal primary diagnosis category prediction.
  Materials and Methods: We constructed a MIMIC-IV cohort of 13,645 admissions from the 10 most frequent primary ICD-10 codes, consolidated into seven categories. Structured variables were serialized into clinical narratives and combined with leakage-pruned discharge notes. Using a frozen MedFound-Llama3-8B-finetuned backbone, we extracted hidden states from five transformer layers and trained linear probes for structured-only, unstructured-only, and combined inputs, comparing against XGBoost and information-matched PLM-ICD baselines and evaluating MIMIC-III adaptation with a compact bottleneck adapter.
  Results: The combined probe performed best on MIMIC-IV (87.69% strict; 91.45% medical accuracy), exceeding both single-modality probes and baselines. The structured-only probe outperformed its standard baseline by 6.19 points in medical accuracy. Diagnostic information became increasingly linearly separable in deeper layers, and a 2M-parameter adapter restored cross-dataset transfer to MIMIC-III using only 5% of target labels.
  Discussion: LLM embeddings can unify structured and narrative EHR information for multimodal diagnosis prediction, supporting efficient reuse of clinical representations across modalities and datasets through a small representation-level module.
  Conclusion: Multimodal probing of frozen medical LLM representations provides a practical approach for studying EHR modalities and adapting clinical representations across datasets.

</details>


### [137] [MedEvoEval: Evaluating Continual Evolution of Doctor Agents through Simulated Clinical Episodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28900)
*Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedEvoEval 是一个基于可执行模拟门诊 episodes 的纵向评估框架，支持评估医生智能体在多轮诊疗中的决策过程与跨 episode 经验演化能力。包含700个处理后的 episode 和完整工具链，实验表明其能揭示过程成本、分析记忆演化、跨任务迁移与能力保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估体系（如固定输入 QA 基准和交互式基准）仅覆盖单轮答案或单次 encounter，无法评估医生智能体在完整门诊 episode 中的多步决策过程，以及跨 episode 的经验积累、行为迁移和能力保留等纵向特性。

Method: 将每个病例转化为角色化视图（患者、检查、管理者），证据仅通过有效动作揭示；每个 episode 记录结构化轨迹（观察、动作、输出、评分、经验回写）；构建包含700个 episode 的可运行 E&D 工件，配套 episode 运行器、评分脚本、配置、日志和轨迹分析代码。

Result: episode 轨迹暴露了最终答案评分隐藏的过程成本；MDT 式会诊可重新分配资源；支持记忆成熟度、留出迁移、更新阶段响应和反向保留等纵向分析。

Conclusion: MedEvoEval 为评估医生智能体是否通过经验改进、行为迁移和长期能力保留提供了具体可执行的评估基础。

Abstract: Doctor agents are moving beyond single-turn answer generation toward evolving clinical decision systems. Within an outpatient episode, they acquire evidence, use examination and consultation resources, and decide when to finalize a diagnosis and management plan. Across episodes, their behavior may change through memory, retrieval, reflection, or other update mechanisms. Current evaluations only partially cover this setting. Fixed-input medical QA benchmarks score final answers from complete inputs, whereas many interactive benchmarks still focus on individual encounters or fixed runs, providing limited support for evaluating how episode-level decisions interact with cross-episode experience. We introduce MedEvoEval, an executable longitudinal evaluation framework based on action-gated simulated outpatient episodes. Each source case is converted into role-specific patient, examination, and manager views; evidence is revealed only through valid actions; and each episode records a structured trace that links observations, actions, final outputs, manager scores, and optional experience write-back. We release a runnable E&D artifact with 700 processed episodes, provenance notes, schemas, an episode runner, scoring scripts, configurations, example logs, analysis code, and trajectory- and step-level derivatives. Experiments show that episode traces expose process costs hidden by final-answer scoring, show how MDT-style consultation reallocates resources, and support longitudinal analyses of memory maturation, held-out transfer, update-stage response, and backward retention. Together, these results show that MedEvoEval provides a concrete basis for evaluating whether doctor agents improve through experience, transfer useful behavior, and retain earlier capabilities over time.

</details>


### [138] [Expert Evaluation of Clinical AI Tools on Real Point-of-Care Clinical Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28960)
*Jean Feng,Vishal Patel,Patrick Heagerty,Yifan Mai,Venkatesh Sivaraman,Patrick Vossler,Jialin Ouyang,Anupam B. Jena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于620个真实临床查询和187个HealthBench问题，由149名执业医师盲法评估专业AI工具（OpenEvidence）与三个通用大模型（Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.5）在准确性、临床实用性、来源质量、可验证性和完整性五个维度的表现。专业工具在所有维度上均显著优于通用模型，胜率差距达25-39个百分点（p<0.001）。研究同时发布了Real-POCQi公共基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI工具在临床领域主要基于假设性或考试型问题进行评估，缺乏对医生实际工作中提出的真实查询的评估。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过真实临床查询评估AI工具的实际表现。

Method: 收集620个来自OpenEvidence平台的真实临床查询（Real-POCQi）及187个HealthBench问题，由149名覆盖36个州、30个专科的执业医师进行盲法头对头比较，在五个维度（准确性、临床实用性、来源质量、可验证性、完整性）上评估专业工具与三个通用大模型的表现。

Result: 专业工具在所有五个维度上均得分最高；在Real-POCQi的主要分析中，胜率差距为25-39个百分点（p<0.001）。结果在引用显示、答案长度、OE用户状态等敏感性分析中保持一致。LLM评判员与专家评判员存在系统性差异，但两者对最佳模型的判断基本一致。

Conclusion: AI工具的评估应反映真实世界查询分布并使用与专科匹配的专家评判员；专业工具相较于通用模型的持续优势表明，针对性的工程化和定制化可带来有意义的性能提升。

Abstract: Physicians now pose millions of clinical questions to AI tools each week, yet these tools are evaluated largely on hypothetical or exam-style questions, not those actually asked in practice. We report a blinded evaluation built on 620 Real-world Point-Of-Care Queries (Real-POCQi) submitted to the OpenEvidence (OE) platform by physicians spanning 30 specialties, as well as 187 questions from HealthBench. 149 practicing physicians across 36 states made head-to-head comparisons between answers from three frontier general-purpose models (Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.5) and a specialized clinical tool (OE), with graders matched to each question's specialty. When comparing answers along five dimensions relevant to clinical decision support -- accuracy, clinical utility, source quality, verifiability, & completeness -- physicians scored the specialized tool highest on all axes; in the primary analysis on Real-POCQi, win differences (margins between win and loss rates) ranged from 25 to 39 percentage points (p<0.001). Results remained consistent in sensitivity analyses stratifying by citation display, answer length, OE-user status, and Real-POCQi versus HealthBench. In parallel, LLM judges were found to systematically differ from expert judges, though both generally agreed on the best model. These findings underscore two conclusions: (i) AI tool evaluations should reflect real-world query distributions and use expert judges that mirror the specialization defining modern medicine and (ii) the consistent advantage of the specialized tool over general-purpose models does not necessarily mean that the latter cannot serve similar purposes, but that targeted engineering and customization can yield meaningful gains in performance for its users. We release Real-POCQi as a public benchmark, as well as the prespecified statistical analysis for reproducing results of this study.

</details>


### [139] [Customized Generative AI Agent for Transportation Engineering Practice: A Development and Continued Pre-training Guideline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29014)
*Dianwei Chen,Yuan-Zheng Lei,Zifan Zhang,Yuchen Liu,Xianfeng,Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LoRA微调框架，利用美国交通手册和技术文档对六个大语言模型进行持续预训练，开发面向交通工程领域的专用生成式AI代理，其中Qwen2.5-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在交通工程等专业工程领域因缺乏技术标准、工程术语和领域语义的充分训练，效果有限。

Method: 构建美国交通手册、设计指南和监管文件的语料库，通过统一的低秩适配（LoRA）框架对六个SOTA大语言模型进行持续预训练。

Result: Qwen2.5-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B在领域对齐和响应质量上表现最佳，LoRA适配有效提升了模型在技术内容理解和上下文特定推理上的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个可复现的领域专用生成式AI代理开发框架，支持交通研究、设计、规划和政策分析等领域的更广泛部署。

Abstract: Recent advancements in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and large language models (LLMs) have shown significant promise in automating complex reasoning, summarization, and question-answering tasks. However, the effectiveness of general-purpose LLMs in specialized engineering domains remains limited due to insufficient exposure to technical standards, engineering terminology, and domain-specific semantics. This study proposes a systematic approach to developing a customized generative AI agent for transportation engineering applications. A curated corpus of U.S. transportation manuals, design guidelines, and regulatory documents is used to conduct continued pretraining of six state-of-the-art LLMs through a unified low-rank adaptation (LoRA) framework. The training process is monitored to ensure convergence and model stability. Performance is evaluated using standard natural language processing metrics, including BLEU-4 and ROUGE, with Qwen2.5-7B and LLaMA-3.1-8B demonstrating the highest domain alignment and response quality. Results validate the effectiveness of LoRA-based adaptation in improving LLM performance on technical content interpretation and context-specific reasoning. This work contributes a reproducible development framework for constructing domain-specialized generative AI agents, supporting broader deployment in transportation research, design, planning, and policy analysis.

</details>


### [140] [Preventing Error Propagation in Multi-Agent AI through Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29026)
*Shahnewaz Karim Sakib,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体AI系统中的推理交换与答案修正机制，发现该方法在多数情况下能提升准确率，但也存在错误传播的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体通过交换推理轨迹和修正答案可能提升性能，但同时也可能引入可靠性风险（正确智能体被误导）。现有研究对此权衡缺乏系统分析。

Method: 构建框架：智能体先独立回答多选题，再共享推理轨迹并修正答案。在网络安全、网络工程、常识等多个领域进行数值实验，评估准确率提升、正向/负向答案转换比等指标。

Result: 多智能体推理交换能提升整体准确率，正向答案转换（纠正错误）多于负向转换（误导正确），但效果因领域而异，部分场景下存在错误传播现象。

Conclusion: 多智能体推理交换在提升可靠性方面总体有效，但需注意特定情境下的错误传播风险，为设计更鲁棒的多智能体通信机制提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems can improve answer selection by allowing different language models to exchange reasoning traces, revise initial predictions, and support a final decision. However, such communication may also introduce reliability risks: reasoning from one agent can correct another agent's mistake, but it can also mislead an agent that was initially correct. This paper studies reliable multi-agent AI communication through reasoning exchange and runtime answer revision. We develop a framework in which agents first answer multiple-choice questions independently, then share reasoning traces and revise their decisions. We conduct numerical experiments where we evaluate whether this process improves accuracy, produces more positive than negative answer transitions, and remains effective across domains such as cybersecurity, networking, and general knowledge. The results help identify when multi-agent reasoning improves reliability and when it may propagate errors.

</details>


### [141] [Memory as an Attack Surface in LLM Agents: A Study on Multiple-Choice Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29030)
*Shahnewaz Karim Sakib,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了LLM智能体在多项选择题中存在记忆操纵漏洞：通过注入误导性记忆，即使当前问题内容干净，智能体也容易被引导选择错误答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体虽然通过记忆机制提升了个性化响应能力，但记忆存储的信息可能成为新的攻击面——即使当前查询是干净的，历史记忆中的误导信息仍会污染输出结果，威胁任务可靠性。

Method: 1. 设计并实现带外部记忆组件的LLM智能体（支持存储/检索任务相关信息）；2. 在多项选择问答场景中注入误导性或篡改记忆；3. 通过控制变量实验对比操纵前后的准确率、攻击成功率及被操纵选项的选择率。

Result: 简单的记忆操纵即可显著影响智能体最终答案，导致其在面对格式正确、内容干净的问题时仍选择错误选项。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体的记忆机制存在严重安全漏洞，需要设计更鲁棒的记忆防护策略来抵御数据投毒类的记忆操纵攻击。

Abstract: AI agents extend conventional large language model (LLM) applications by integrating language understanding with task execution, external tool use, and memory mechanisms. While memory allows agents to retain prior interactions and provide more personalized and context-aware responses, it also introduces a new vulnerability: information stored in memory can influence future outputs even when the current query is clean. In this paper, we investigate memory manipulation in LLM-based agents for multiple-choice question answering. We first design and implement an LLM-based AI agent with an external memory component that stores and retrieves task-relevant information. We then introduce basic memory manipulation scenarios in which misleading or corrupted memories are inserted into the agent before it answers multiple-choice questions. Using a controlled experimental setup, we compare the agent's performance before and after memory manipulation and measure changes in answer accuracy, attack success rate, and selection of manipulated options. Our results show that even simple memory manipulations can noticeably affect the agent's final answers, causing it to select incorrect options despite receiving clean and well-formed questions.

</details>


### [142] [Low-cost concept-based localized explanations: How far can we get with training-free approaches?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29069)
*Darian Fernández-Gutiérrez,Rafael Bello,Marilyn Bello,Natalia Díaz-Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本概念命名（CoNa）协议，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对图像区域进行概念标注，无需训练即可实现62%-88%的物体级准确率，为低成本概念可解释AI研究提供了可复现框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念可解释AI（C-XAI）需要细粒度概念标注来进行验证，但人工标注稀缺且成本高昂，现有方法缺乏在零样本条件下对局部区域进行概念命名的评估体系。

Method: 提出零样本概念命名协议CoNa，包括：（i）封闭集、类别受限提示用于中等词汇量；（ii）Open-CoNa，基于嵌入相似度的策略用于大标签空间。在4个MLLM（7B-32B）上进行实验。

Result: 在物体级概念命名上达到62%-88%的精确匹配准确率，多个MLLM在不同数据集上表现出稳定一致的趋势。

Conclusion: 训练自由的概念标注从局部区域中展现出潜力，但仍有局限性和失败模式，发布的框架可支持未来低成本的C-XAI研究。

Abstract: Concept-based Explainable AI (C-XAI) seeks human-understandable explanations grounded in semantic concepts, yet validation is limited by the scarcity of fine-grained concept annotations. We evaluate whether mid-scale Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can perform localized concept naming under strict zero-shot conditions by assigning labels to bounding-box regions at both object and part levels. We propose a reproducible zero-shot evaluation protocol for Concept Naming (CoNa) with (i) closed-set, category-constrained prompting for moderate vocabularies and (ii) Open-CoNa, an embedding-similarity-based strategy for large label spaces. Experiments with four MLLMs (7B-32B) show consistent performance trends across datasets, reaching 62%-88% object-level exact-match accuracy, highlighting the potential of training-free concept annotation from localized regions. We discuss limitations and failure modes and release a reproducible framework to support future low-cost C-XAI research.

</details>


### [143] [Managing the Human Fallback: Skill Investment Under Improving AI and Worker Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29111)
*Simrita Singh,Naireet Ghosh,Tinglong Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 企业部署自主AI时，需权衡系统自动化与人工参与度，这一决策影响当前产出与未来人力资本。研究发现，在单一企业基准下，参与度仅作为备用投资有价值，最不熟练的工人被优先投入；但在工人流动性存在时，参与模式可能逆转，投资从最不熟练转向接近AI基准的最熟练工人。AI进步的两个维度（能力与可靠性）对参与度的影响也不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究企业在部署自主AI时，如何决定系统自动化与人工参与度的分配，以及这一决策对当前产出和未来人力资本（技能积累与流失）的影响。

Method: 构建一个简约的两期模型，假设AI在正常运行时表现优于工人，但存在故障概率。企业选择工人参与度（engagement），参与度降低当前产出（对低于基准的工人），但通过学习和流失改变未来技能。区分AI进步的两个维度：能力（正常运行时产出）和可靠性（正常运行的概率）。

Result: 单一企业基准下，参与度仅作为备用投资有价值，企业最倾向于让最不熟练的工人参与。存在工人流动性时，工人偏好能构建更有价值技能轨迹的工作，这一筛选动机使投资转向接近AI基准的高技能工人，可能逆转参与模式。AI能力提升通过增加技能轨迹价值提高参与度；可靠性提升则因减少备用需求并改变学习机会，对参与度的影响不确定。

Conclusion: 工人流动性下，人-AI工作设计本质上是人力资本投资问题，当前的工作分配塑造未来技能。AI进步的不同维度对参与度产生差异化影响，企业的投资决策需考虑劳动力市场中的工人选择行为。

Abstract: When firms deploy autonomous AI, they must decide how much work to leave to the system and how much to keep workers engaged. This decision affects current output and future human capital. We develop a parsimonious two-period model in which AI may outperform the worker when it functions, but may fail with positive probability. A firm chooses worker engagement; engagement lowers current output for below-benchmark workers, but changes future skill through learning and erosion. We distinguish two dimensions of AI progress: capability, the system's output when it works, and reliability, the probability that it works. In a single-firm benchmark, engagement is valuable only as fallback investment. The firm engages the least-skilled workers most, because they have the largest skill gaps and are least costly to bring toward a useful fallback level. With worker mobility, engagement also affects labor-market sorting: workers prefer jobs that build more valuable skill trajectories. This sorting motive targets higher-skill workers near the AI frontier, where skill gains are more valuable and engagement is less costly. Mobility can therefore reverse the engagement pattern, shifting investment from the least-skilled toward the most-skilled workers below the AI benchmark. Mobility also reshapes how AI progress affects engagement: greater capability raises engagement by increasing the value of the skill trajectory a firm offers, whereas greater reliability can raise or lower it because it reduces fallback need while also changing learning opportunities. Under worker mobility, human-AI work design becomes a problem of human-capital investment, in which allocating work today shapes future skill.

</details>


### [144] [Characterizing Large Language Model Agentic Workflows: A Study on N8n Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29116)
*Yutian Tang,Yuming Zhou,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实证研究了低代码自动化平台（n8n）中超过6,000个LLM代理工作流，发现LLM并非简单提示响应管道，而是嵌入包含控制逻辑、外部工具和人工审核点的复杂自动化结构中。同时揭示了可靠性机制（如回退路径、修复循环、失败告警和人工审批）仍普遍缺失的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正快速被低代码/无代码平台采用，但缺乏对实际LLM代理工作流的大规模实证研究，不了解其真实设计模式、结构特征和可靠性现状。

Method: 收集并分析了n8n平台上超过6,000个公开可用的LLM工作流，从任务分布、结构与工具使用模式、可靠性机制、自主性水平四个维度进行实证分析。

Result: LLM工作流并非简单提示管道，而是嵌入更广泛的自动化结构（控制逻辑、外部工具、通信服务、存储系统、人工审核点）。虽然许多工作流包含轻量级后处理或路由逻辑，但结构化回退路径、修复循环、失败告警和人工审批等显式可靠性机制仍相对罕见。

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM代理在实际自动化生态系统中日益增长的部署与可靠性、安全性和治理方面工程支持不足之间的差距，并提出了10项实证发现和5项研究建议。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly being adopted in low-code and no-code automation platforms, where non-expert users design workflows that combine natural language understanding with external services and APIs. LLM agents are LLM systems that use LLMs as a core "brain" to reason, plan, and autonomously execute complex, multi-step tasks. In this paper, we present the first large-scale empirical study of LLM agentic workflows in low-code automation platforms. We analyze more than 6,000 publicly available n8n workflows and examine four aspects of their design: task distribution, structural and tool use patterns, reliability mechanisms, and autonomy levels. Our analysis shows that LLM workflows are not merely prompt response pipelines. Instead, LLMs are commonly embedded within broader automation structures involving control logic, external tools, communication services, storage systems, and human review points. We further find that while many workflows include lightweight post-processing or routing logic after LLM execution, explicit reliability mechanisms such as structured fallback paths, repair loops, failure-specific alerts, and human approval gates remain relatively uncommon. These results reveal a gap between the increasing deployment of LLM agents in practical automation ecosystems and the limited engineering support for reliability, safety, and governance. Overall, our study provides ten empirical findings and five research takeaways for researchers, platform developers, and practitioners seeking to understand and improve real-world LLM agentic workflows.

</details>


### [145] [HiComm: Hierarchical Communication for Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29126)
*Runze Zhao,Dongruo Zhou,Sumit Kumar Jha,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Ankit Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiComm是一种即插即用的通信模块，通过将消息 grounded 在发送者的分层观测结构中，实现从非结构化向量传输到结构化信息检索的转变，在降低23倍通信量的同时匹配或超越现有基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL通信协议将消息视为与观测结构无关的扁平密集向量，忽略了协作环境中观测天然具有的层次结构（如组和实体）这一重要归纳偏置。

Method: 接收者驱动的通信机制：接收者发出查询，通过三级解码（先选组→再选发送者→最后选组内实体）从发送者的分层观测中检索对应特征切片作为消息，采用Straight-Through Gumbel-Softmax实现可微分离散选择。

Result: 在具有不同观测结构和协调需求的协作MARL任务中，HiComm匹配或超越了代表性学习通信基线，同时每个接收者每个episode的通信量减少高达23倍。

Conclusion: 将通信 grounded 在观测的层次结构中，可以在保持性能的同时大幅降低通信开销，为MARL通信设计提供了新的结构化方向。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) often relies on communication to mitigate partial observability, yet most existing protocols treat messages as flat dense vectors detached from the structure of the observations they summarize. This design overlooks an important source of inductive bias in many cooperative environments, where observations naturally follow a hierarchy such as groups and entities. We propose \textsc{HiComm}, a plug-in communication module that grounds messages in the sender's hierarchical observation. \textsc{HiComm} is receiver-driven: the receiver issues a query, and the hierarchy is resolved through a three-stage decoding process that first selects a group, then a sender, and then an entity within that group, returning the corresponding feature slice as the message. This converts communication from unstructured vector transmission into structured information retrieval over the sender's observation hierarchy. We instantiate this mechanism with Straight-Through Gumbel-Softmax for differentiable discrete selection and a lightweight shared projection design that attaches to standard MARL pipelines. Experiments across cooperative MARL tasks with different observation structures and coordination demands show that \textsc{HiComm} matches or outperforms representative learned communication baselines while reducing communication volume by up to $23\times$ per receiver per episode.

</details>


### [146] [Flow Reasoning Models: Scaling Reasoning Through Iterative Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29150)
*Alec Helbling,Andrey Bryutkin,Mauro Martino,Nima Dehmamy,Hendrik Strobelt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Reasoning Models (FRMs)，利用流模型自身的去噪动态稳定性作为验证器，通过测试时扩展和训练优化，在数独和Zebra谜题等结构化推理任务上实现了接近完美的高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流模型在少步文本生成上表现良好，但在结构化推理任务（如数独、Zebra谜题）中容易收敛到错误答案，求解率仅约36%，亟需提升其推理能力。

Method: 1) 观察到流模型可作为自身验证器：正确答案是去噪动态的稳定不动点；2) 测试时扩展：生成多个候选解，保留动态稳定的解；3) 训练改进：引入自条件化通道并在推理时关闭，使用直接偏好优化避免失败的生成。

Result: Sudoku-Shah接近100%，Zebra 95.9%，Sudoku-Extreme 96.1%（无需训练该分布）；仅需7次前向传播达到99.2%准确率，比masked-diffusion基线快8倍以上。

Conclusion: FRMs通过结合流模型的自验证特性、测试时扩展和针对性训练，实现了在结构化推理任务上的高效高准确率求解，并展现出良好的分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Discrete flow models have recently shown promising performance on few-step text generation; however, when naively applied to structured reasoning tasks such as Sudoku and Zebra puzzles, they converge confidently to incorrect answers (solving only $\sim$36% of Sudoku puzzles). We introduce Flow Reasoning Models (FRMs), a training and test-time-scaling framework for structured reasoning with flow models. We make the observation that, despite their poor solve rate, flow models can act as their own verifiers. A correct answer is a stable fixed point of the denoising dynamics, returning to itself when re-noised and re-solved. This enables a test-time-scaling paradigm: propose many candidate solutions and keep those that are dynamically stable, which alone reaches high solve rates on Sudoku-Shah (~$100\%$) and Zebra ($95.9\%$). This even generalizes to harder out-of-distribution puzzles like Sudoku-Extreme ($96.1\%$), without ever training on that distribution. This pure search, however, wastes a great deal of computation generating incorrect candidate solutions. We therefore design a training recipe to improve the base model's efficiency. First, we train flow models with a self-conditioning channel and close it at inference, letting them refine their own past predictions. Second, we train models to avoid their own failed generations using direct preference optimization. These changes substantially improve the base model's efficiency, letting it reach $99.2\%$ on Sudoku in just $7$ forward passes, over $8\times$ fewer than the strongest matched masked-diffusion baseline we compare needs for the same accuracy. When combined with test-time scaling, this lets flow models solve hard out-of-distribution puzzles (e.g. Sudoku-Extreme) far more efficiently.

</details>


### [147] [Pooled Leaderboards Hide System-Specific Winners: A Reporting-Protocol Audit of Offline Root-Cause Analysis Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29159)
*Lining Hu,Ting Liu,Yuzhuo Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对三个开源RCA基准测试（OpenRCA、RCAEval、PetShop）进行审计，发现这些基准通常报告单一的整体Top-1准确率，但实际在不同子系统间效果差异显著。通过配对比较四种方法，所有六组对比均表现出子系统层面的正负双向效应，随机效应预测区间均跨越零，留一系统外选择在11个保留子系统中最多有5个选择了得分更低的方法，最大遗憾值达24.8个百分点。作者发布了一个320行的审计模块用于复现这些稳定性检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线RCA基准测试通常仅报告单一的整体Top-1准确率，工程师会据此为自身子系统选择方法，但这种方法在不同子系统间的表现可能存在显著差异，需要进行审计验证。

Method: 对OpenRCA、RCAEval和PetShop三个基准家族的11个子系统和778个匹配评分单元进行审计，使用BARO、CD-1min适配器、max-|Z|和每服务告警计数四种方法进行六组配对比较，采用随机效应模型和留一系统外选择等统计方法进行分析。

Result: 所有六组配对比较均显示子系统层面的正负双向效应，每个随机效应95%预测区间均跨越零，案例层面交互测试在5/6配对中拒绝可交换性假设。留一系统外选择在11个保留子系统中最多有5个选择了得分较低的方法，最大遗憾值达24.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 基准测试的单一整体准确率排名具有误导性，不能简单推荐给所有子系统。作者发布了审计模块以帮助研究者和工程师进行更稳健的跨子系统性能评估。

Abstract: Offline root-cause-analysis (RCA) benchmarks commonly rank methods by a single pooled top-1 accuracy across multiple subsystems, and engineers often read the pooled winner as a recommendation for their own subsystem. We audit that reading on three public RCA benchmark families -- OpenRCA, RCAEval, and PetShop -- covering 11 subsystems and 778 matched scoring units. To keep pairwise comparisons on identical cases, the main analysis retains four methods or comparators with complete coverage: BARO, a CD-1min adapter, max-$|Z|$, and per-service alert-count. All six pairwise comparisons show subsystem-level effects of both signs, every random-effects 95\% prediction interval crosses zero, and case-level interaction tests reject exchangeability in 5 of 6 pairs. Leave-one-system-out selection picks the lower-scoring method on up to 5 of 11 held-out subsystems, with regret reaching 24.8 pp on RCAEval / Sock-Shop. We release a 320-line audit module; given a matched RCA benchmark score table, it recomputes the same per-subsystem stability checks alongside pooled scores.

</details>


### [148] [Direct Causation in International Humanitarian Law and the Challenge of AI-Mediated Civilian Cyber Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29175)
*Alice Saito,Harold Godsoe,Phan Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文分析了AI中介的平民网络行动对国际人道法中“直接参与敌对行动”因果标准的挑战，指出自主多智能体系统因决策后人类脱钩和下游行为者缺失导致“直接因果关系”判断失效，并提出了目标细化粒度作为关键属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有国际人道法关于平民直接参与敌对行动的因果测试标准，在AI自主网络行动场景下出现结构性适用困难，亟需理论澄清。

Method: 通过规范分析与技术场景结合，区分“一步因果”与“整体部分”两个子标准，提出五级AI中介行动谱系分类，并评估现行AI治理工具的不足。

Result: 自主多智能体系统的决策后人类脱钩使“一步因果”标准失效；“整体部分”要求因缺乏下游人类主体而无法适用；目标细化粒度是影响“具体性”判断的关键但未被现有技术治理工具记录。

Conclusion: 现行框架倾向于将此类部署视为间接参与，与规则初衷相悖；现有AI治理工具在关键属性记录上存在空白，需制度补充。

Abstract: International humanitarian law protects civilians from direct attack unless and for such time as they take direct part in hostilities, with the ICRC's 2009 Interpretive Guidance operationalising this rule through a three-criterion cumulative test. This paper argues that AI-mediated civilian cyber operations challenge the direct causation element of this test in a structurally specific way: when a civilian deploys an autonomous multi-agent cyber system of the kind recently demonstrated in offensive AI research, the "one causal step" standard fails because harm is produced by system-generated decisions made after human disengagement, and the integral-part requirement does not extend because it presupposes downstream human contributors whose conduct can be independently classified. The framework therefore defaults to treating such deployments as indirect participation, in tension with its purpose of capturing civilians who personally take part in hostilities. Beyond the doctrinal analysis, this paper identifies goal-specification granularity as the property on which the integral-part test's concreteness component implicitly turns, classifies AI-mediated operations along a five-level spectrum, and argues that existing technical AI governance instruments do not log or report this property.

</details>


### [149] [Selective Memory Retention for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29178)
*Pranath Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TraceRetain框架，研究在受限于容量的外部记忆中，何时以及如何保留条目能提升大语言模型智能体的任务表现。实验表明，在干净数据下不同保留策略差异不显著，但在噪声干扰下TraceRetain能保持稳定性能，而无限记忆和FIFO策略明显退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型大语言模型智能体通常采用无限记忆或简单缓存策略，缺乏对记忆条目进行智能筛选和淘汰的机制。作者希望探究在有限容量的外部记忆下，何种保留策略能有效抵御记忆污染（如噪声干扰），从而提升大语言模型智能体的鲁棒性和任务成功率。

Method: 提出了TraceRetain框架，这是一个轻量级的有界外部记忆管理系统。框架通过多个可解释特征（任务成功与否、条目年龄、访问频率、冗余度、特异性、相似度、下游效用）对记忆条目进行评分，并在达到容量上限时淘汰得分最低的条目。在清洁ALFWorld环境和引入75%合成干扰器的噪声环境下进行评估。
